the Lua side now hands Java your keys, the world's Sandbox rules (every option on the page, under its own name) and the skill state through separate calls with new names; the safe fall height's Sandbox option is now safeFall (a world that had changed it needs it set again); fall damage is simply on or off plus that height; the wall, window, door and fence checks share one helper (Sides); deploy, workshop and liveWorkshop now also remove files that are no longer part of the mod; build 165:the animation clips no longer carry a copy of the game's character mesh: each keeps only the skeleton it moves (its frames, and the skinned bones with their bind matrices) on a stand-in mesh of one small triangle per bone, which the game's Assimp reads exactly as before (the same nodes, bones, bind matrices and keys), and the mod is about 6 MB smaller; the clip generators write clips that way and tools/StandInRig.java converts old ones; the build script is rewritten, and the mod's own source is published in its folder under source/; build 164:the Freerunner's starting Parkour level is no longer a Sandbox option: the Freerunner always starts at level 10 with every move unlocked; build 163:panic now matters far less the higher your Parkour level, as rain and snow do: full panic is a 12% slip at level 0, about 6% at level 5 and well under 1% at level 10, where parkour is second nature (tired, drunk, in pain and loaded still only halve by level 10); build 162:gameplay settings moved to the Sandbox options (a Project Viewpoint Parkour page, the same for everyone in a world and set by the admin on a server): which moves are on, walkable roofs, the safe fall height, no fall damage, carry weight and its impact; new there: a Parkour XP multiplier, every move unlocked at any level, the Freerunner's starting Parkour level (10 by default), and switches for ankle injuries, the slip chance, crowd trips from the shove and the drop kick's recovery by level; Options > Mods keeps only the keys; a server scales its Parkour XP limits with the multiplier and grants the Freerunner the Sandbox's level; rain and snow slips now depend strongly on your Parkour level: twice as likely as before at level 0, a little over half of that at level 5 and a tenth at level 10 (the rest of the slip chance still halves by level 10); build 161:balance: falls are free up to three storeys by default (was four), and a landing past that can sprain or break an ankle (a fracture or a painful foot), likelier and worse the further past it; a ledge grab can slip and drop you, and an auto-vault can end in the game's own trip, when you are tired, panicked, drunk, in pain, heavily loaded, or out in the rain or snow (Slip; a higher Parkour level makes it rarer, and the vault only adds panic and the weather to the game's own trip chance); after a drop kick you stay on the floor longer below Parkour level 10, 0.3 s more per level (the landing clip is held at its lying part); below Parkour level 7 the running shove can trip you in a crowd of three or more (the game's own running stumble, 6% per level under 7); the Freerunner costs 6 points, keeps Parkour 10 and every move, gets Sprinting +2, Lightfooted +2 and Nimble +1, half-cost parkour moves and a quarter off running instead of free moves and 70% off, and carries 15% less, eats a lot and heals slowly (Hearty Appetite, Slow Healer) instead of Sunday Driver; build 160:jumping or walking off the end of a roof at an angle no longer hits an invisible wall at the gable end: the game also checks the square half a tile ahead along your heading, near a corner that is the square across the corner, and the gable pass refused those, so you stopped dead before the edge; the trim along a gable (roof pieces with a wall flag) no longer spoils the pass, a roof tile whose shape stops short of its edge still gives the gable its height, and rising in a jump counts the height you gain before you reach the edge; when the game stops you up on a roof or in the air the log says which check it was, what stands on that edge by name and why the roof rules did not let you through (roofs: the game stopped you), and a mid-air stop names what you were heading for; build 159:the trim along the gable edges of roofs (roofs_accents_*) no longer counts as roof: its tiles borrow a box 3.45 m tall standing on its edge, which made a solid wall of roof a whole storey high along every trimmed gable, so you can now step and jump off those gable ends; any such upright board in a roof tile's shape is left out too; build 158:sheet ropes climb like ladders: walk or jump into one, or let the game start its own rope climb (its Climb option, climbing out of a window onto one) and you get the rope, W and S climb with the game's rope animation set from your height, holding still holds on, at the top the game climbs you in through the window, frame or railing; heavy things in your hands are dropped and your arms tire as in the game, no falling off; a ladder or rope is taken hold of a little to one side of it too (it no longer has to be on your own square), and from a little further off the wall; the ladder facing lock no longer switches itself off after swinging onto a ladder from above; up on a roof, being stopped while pushing a key logs what is on the edge ahead and what each rule made of it (roofs: STUCK), as does every 'a roof is in your way'; build 157:ladders have their own climbing animation (Bob_ParkourLadder, tools/GenLadderClip.java): hand over hand and foot over foot, opposite hand and foot together, two rungs at a time; its frame follows your height on the ladder, so the hands and feet stay on the rungs the ladder tiles draw whether you climb up, down or hold on; a hand or foot taking a rung makes a sound; at the top you lean in before the climb-up; build 156:ladders: walk up to one and take hold, W climbs up, S climbs down, standing still holds on, the top climbs over onto the roof or floor beyond, the bottom steps off, walking or backing over the edge above a ladder's top swings you onto it, Space jumps off, Ctrl or C lets go; the game's own climbing clip, following your speed; every ladder the game's tiles name (ladderN/S/W/E), including the ones its rope climb used to take (Options: Ladders); build 155:the ledge grab latches onto the edge of any floor or roof above you even with no wall under it (balconies, gas station canopies, flat roof overhangs under their gutters, porch roofs, stairwell openings) and climbs up and over a balcony's railing; which ledge you reach follows your hands, so a lunge gets you the next storey; jumping into a sheet rope or a climbable ladder hands you to the game's own climb; the wall scramble is now a lunge on top of the jump, higher; the leap frog carries you forward until you land, and other air moves can take over once you are past the zombie; build 154:sloped roofs are solid in Viewpoint's 3D views: you stand, walk, run and slide on the slope instead of inside it, land on roofs, climb onto them with a ledge grab and step off their edges and gable ends; a roof's side blocks like a wall and its underside stops a jump (Options: Walkable roofs); build 153: the wallrun now needs a real jump at running speed, aimed at the wall from a shallow angle while you look toward it (running past a wall, jumping away from it, and walking or falling off a roof edge beside it no longer start one), and the fast sprint stays off while you are limping, hurt in a leg or foot, in pain, or the game says you cannot sprint; build 152: renamed from ParkourMod to ProjectViewpointParkour, covering the mod id, the jar, the Lua files and registry ids, the folders and this log prefix; build 151: latching onto a ledge makes a sound to match what you grabbed: the game's fence sound on metal fences and gates, its wood sound on wooden fences, a plain step on every other ledge; build 150: the carry weight slider's label no longer has a percent sign in it (the Mods options page failed to build in Korean at that slider); build 149: wall scramble works on every plain wall that rises above your feet and its clip stays on while you cling to it, a third jump on a tall wall kicks you off backwards, scramble refusals are logged; build 148: wallrun footsteps, hands-on-wall pose back in third person (isometric view: no hands-on-wall pose, no auto-vault, no wall stumble), auto-vault leaves staircase railings and ladder tops alone, Parkour XP and the Freerunner's starting level awarded by the server in multiplayer, fall and falling-pose hooks only touch your own character, nothing of ours moves you for a moment after leaving a vehicle, dashes stop at cars)
